EPAR summary of EPAR reports intended for publicity
Ifirmasta1 irbesartanum
to the European public assessment report (Europe Public Assessment Report, EPAR) for Ifirmasta.
explains how the Committee for Medicinal Products (CHMP) assessed this medicine to develop its opinion on the authorisation and recommendations on the conditions of use of Ifirmasta.
, what is Ifirmast?
to Ifirmasta is a medicinal product that contains the active substance irbesartan.
in the form of tablets (75, 150 and 300 mg).
to Ifirmasta is ‘generic medicine’.
to say that Ifirmasta is similar to ‘reference medicinal product’, which is already registered in the European Union, namely Aprovel.
more information on generics can be found in the relevant document and answers here.
to what is Ifirmasta used?
to Ifirmasta is used in adults with essential hypertension (high blood pressure).
‘Sixential’ means that hypertension has no obvious cause.
to Ifirmasta is also used to treat kidney disease in adults with hypertension and diabetes 2. type.
